## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单: gpu-6000pro-2 的 **cuda:0, cuda:1** 仅此两张，绝不触碰其他卡
- OLMo 模型路径必须用 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/olmo-1b-hf/`（不是 `olmo-1b`）
- DCOL margin_lr ≤ 1e-4（1e-3 导致 embedding collapse，已验证）
- Qwen2.5 系列不可用（代码占比过高，LoRA 无法覆盖，已验两轮）
- DCOL 必须用 LoRA 微调（D008: 全参微调 DeBERTa 会崩溃，step 340 acc 40→32%）
- `submit_training_job` 命令必须自带 `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`，不能假设 .venv

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- **服务器**: gpu-6000pro-2（2× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 98GB）
- **工作目录**: `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est`
- **Python**: `/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base env，无 venv）
- **模型路径**: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/{pythia-1.4b, gpt2-xl, olmo-1b-hf, deberta-v3-large}/`
- **数据**: `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/data/` (depth_0.jsonl 5000 seed + 各模型子目录)
- **HF cache**: `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache`
- **活跃进程**: gpt2xl_topk50_001 刚结束（ANALYZING），两张 GPU 当前可能空闲
- **活跃 Worker (6个)**:
  - `worker_ssh_gpt2xl_topk50_eval` — 分析 gpt2xl_topk50_001 结果
  - `worker_ssh_dcol_v4_lora_clean_restart` — 在服务器上准备 DCOL v4 LoRA 环境
  - `worker_code_dcol_deberta_004` — 编写 DCOL v4 LoRA 代码
  - `worker_ssh_bugfix_load_features` — 修复 load_features 相关 bug
  - `worker_ssh_zeroshot_baseline_dryrun` — zero-shot baseline dry run
  - `worker_ssh_zeroshot_cleanup` — zero-shot 清理

### 3. 📊 当前状态
**刚做完**: 3×3 矩阵 8/9 格完成，gpt2xl_topk50_001 刚跑完进入 ANALYZING，Worker 正在分析。olmo_topk50_001 和 olmo_temp09_001 均已 DONE(success)。DCOL v3 被 kill（全参微调崩溃），转 LoRA 方案（dcol_deberta_004）。

**Registry 关键状态**:
| exp_id | status | 摘要 |
|--------|--------|------|
| gpt2xl_topk50_001 | ANALYZING | 3×3 最后一格，Worker 正在分析 |
| olmo_temp09_002 | PREPARING(killed) | Director 确认无需重跑，可清理 |
| dcol_deberta_003 | DONE(killed) | 全参微调崩溃，已转 LoRA 方案 |

**等待中**:
- 6 个 Worker 回调（gpt2xl eval、DCOL v4 代码+环境、zeroshot baseline、bugfix）
- gpt2xl_topk50_001 分析完 → 3×3 矩阵 9/9 完成 → 向 Director 汇报全矩阵结果
- DCOL v4 代码就绪 → 提交到 cuda:0 或 cuda:1 训练

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- gpt2xl_topk50_eval Worker 回调 → 更新 registry metrics + conclusion → 汇报 Director 3×3 矩阵 9/9 完成
- DCOL v4 代码 Worker 回调 → review 代码 → 提交 dcol_deberta_004 训练（cuda:0 或 cuda:1）
- zeroshot baseline Worker 回调 → 整合 baseline battery 结果
- bugfix Worker 回调 → 验证修复、更新相关脚本
- 3×3 完成 + DCOL v4 提交后 → 开始 Atlas v2 更新（加入 gpt2xl_topk50 数据点）
- DCOL v4 训练完成 → 若 success，与 RF/CORAL/DivEye 对比；若 negative，走 red-section framing 提案
- 全部实验完成 → EDD threshold sensitivity 分析（0.65/0.70/0.75/0.80）

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**核心结论链**:
- D001: MVP PASS，递归深度可区分（Pythia 78.2%）
- D002: Dream 审稿 median 3.0，Atlas/EDD 为 primary contribution，DCOL 为 secondary
- D004→D006: 解码策略影响是模型依赖的（Pythia temp09 降，GPT-2 XL/OLMo temp09 升）
- D007: Feature importance "consistent set" 而非 "single best"
- D008: DCOL 全参微调不可行，转 LoRA
- D009: 6/7 cells EDD=3（未饱和），Pythia topk50 EDD=1（小模型+受限采样→早饱和）
- D010: p90_ppl/var_surprisal 随 depth 单调递减，rep_ngram 单调递增，两类信号互补

**最佳结果**: GPT-2 XL temp09 85.4% > OLMo temp09 82.03% > GPT-2 XL nucleus 83.3%

**易错点**:
- ROADMAP 里 3×3 矩阵状态是旧的（显示 OLMo temp09 running、topk50 queued），实际 registry 已更新为 DONE
- olmo_temp09_002 是残留条目（killed），不要试图恢复它
- DCOL 已失败 3 次（v1 collapse、v2 OOM、v3 全参崩溃），v4 是 LoRA 方案——如果 v4 也失败，应考虑 DCOL 作为 secondary contribution 是否值得继续投入
- OLMo cron 有 false failure 风险（子进程 PID vs pipeline 主进程），收到 OLMo 失败回调时先 SSH 验证
- Baseline battery 中 CE-MLP 可能训练不足（5 epochs 74.9%），需重跑确认

## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)

## 约束

- Qwen2.5 系列代码占比过高，LoRA 无法覆盖，已验证不可用
- 根分区 ~30GB，模型/数据必须在 `/root/autodl-tmp/`
- OLMo 必须用 `olmo-1b-hf` 目录（不是 `olmo-1b`）
- DCOL margin_lr 不能超过 1e-4（1e-3 导致 embedding collapse）
- OLMo cron 监控有 false failure 风险（子进程 PID vs pipeline 主进程）
- OpenAI SDK base_url: 代码无需修改，v1.0+ 自动读取 OPENAI_BASE_URL 环境变量

{
  "parentUuid": "c8ece7fc-ce3e-4d74-b182-2d1972c5c769",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

d3=13.1%（高于 baseline，top-k 产生更多 code-like text）\\nFiltered 3-class 性能反而略高于 unfiltered (61.3% vs 59.2%)，说明 code contamination 不是性能的主要驱动\\n\\n### 结论\\nSUCCESS — 深度信号在 top-k 采样下仍然存在（69.6% >> 25% chance），但信号强度显著低于 nucleus (-8.6pp) 和 temp09 (-6.65pp)。\\n跨策略泛化成立（信号存在），但不同采样策略对信号强度有影响。Top-k 的截断式采样比连续概率重分配（nucleus/temperature）保留的深度信号更弱。\"}, \"cross_model_gpt2xl_001\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", 0.088)\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\n\\n### Code contamination\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\n\\n### Analysis\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. AUC: 0.7518, d2v3 AUC: 0.6918\\\\n\\\\n### vs Baseline (nucleus p=0.95) 对比\\\\n| Metric | topk50 | baseline | delta |\\\\n|--------|--------|----------|-------|\\\\n| 4-class acc | 69.6% | 78.2% | -8.6pp |\\\\n| 3-class acc | 59.2% | 70.8% | -11.6pp |\\\\n| d0v1 AUC | 0.9927 | 0.9975 | -0.005 |\\\\n| d1v2 AUC | 0.7518 | 0.8607 | -0.109 |\\\\n| d2v3 AUC | 0.6918 | 0.8005 | -0.109 |\\\\n\\\\n### vs temp09 对比\\\\n| Metric | topk50 | temp09 | delta |\\\\n|--------|--------|--------|-------|\\\\n| 4-class acc | 69.6% | 76.25% | -6.65pp |\\\\n| 3-class acc | 59.2% | 69.83% | -10.6pp |\\\\n| d1v2 AUC | 0.7518 | 0.8327 | -0.081 |\\\\n| d2v3 AUC | 0.6918 | 0.7928 | -0.101 |\\\\n\\\\n### Feature importance 一致性\\\\nTop-5 与 baseline 完全一致: var_surprisal, p90_ppl, mean_ppl, mean_surprisal, p75_ppl\\\\n\\\\n### Code contamination (narrow)\\\\nd0=0.4%, d1=7.7%, d2=12.3%, d3=13.1%（高于 baseline，top-k 产生更多 code-like text）\\\\nFiltered 3-class 性能反而略高于 unfiltered (61.3% vs 59.2%)，说明 code contamination 不是性能的主要驱动\\\\n\\\\n### 结论\\\\nSUCCESS — 深度信号在 top-k 采样下仍然存在（69.6% >> 25% chance），但信号强度显著低于 nucleus (-8.6pp) 和 temp09 (-6.65pp)。\\\\n跨策略泛化成立（信号存在），但不同采样策略对信号强度有影响。Top-k 的截断式采样比连续概率重分配（nucleus/temperature）保留的深度信号更弱。\\\"}, \\\"cross_model_gpt2xl_001\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T14:10:51.209Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": +2.7pp |\\\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\\\n\\\\n### Analysis\\\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. \"notes\": \"## Comparison: temp_only τ=0.9 vs baseline nucleus p=0.95\\n\\n### 4-class classification (d0-d3)\\n| Metric | Baseline (nucleus p=0.95) | Temp09 (τ=0.9) | Δ |\\n|--------|--------------------------|-----------------|---|\\n| RF acc | 78.15% [76.9, 79.4] | 76.25% [74.9, 77.5] | -1.9pp |\\n| LR acc | 74.92% | 74.05% | -0.9pp |\\n| AUC 0v1 | 0.9975 | 0.9968 | -0.0007 |\\n| AUC 1v2 | 0.8607 | 0.8327 | -0.028 |\\n| AUC 2v3 | 0.8005 | 0.7928 | -0.008 |\\n\\n### 3-class classification (d1-d3 only)\\n| Metric | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|--------|----------|--------|---|\\n| Full acc | 70.8% | 69.83% | -1.0pp |\\n| Filtered acc | — | 72.52% [70.6, 74.3] | — |\\n| Filtered d1v2 AUC | — | 0.8656 | — |\\n| Filtered d2v3 AUC | — | 0.8295 | — |\\n\\n### Feature importance (top-5, same ranking)\\n1. var_surprisal (0.178 vs 0.169)\\n2. p90_ppl (0.150 vs 0.158)\\n3. mean_ppl (0.114 vs 0.120)\\n4. mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\n\\n### Code contamination\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\n\\n### Analysis\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. Hypothesis confirmed: temperature-only decoding preserves recursive depth signal.\"}, \"mvp_pipeline_pythia_002\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T12:07:25.860Z\", \"updated_at\": 10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）

12	## A. GPU 分配率检查

18

20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备

22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44
